class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">Content type text/plain; charset=utf<span class="hljs-number">-8</span> cannot be simplified to markdown. Here is the raw content<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span>
Contents of https<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span><span class="hljs-comment">//raw.githubusercontent.com/ReZorg/plingua/master/examples/opencog/opencog_pln.pli:</span>
<span class="hljs-comment">/*
 * opencog_pln.pli
 *
 * Probabilistic Logic Networks (PLN) implemented as a pure P-Lingua
 * membrane computing system integrated into the relevance-realization (RR)
 * architecture.
 *
 * PLN is the primary inference engine of OpenCog.  It extends classical
 * logic with uncertain truth values, enabling graded reasoning over the
 * AtomSpace hypergraph.
 *
 * Inference rule families implemented
 * ─────────────────────────────────────
 *   1. Deduction   (A→B, A  ⊢  B)
 *   2. Induction   (A→B, A→C  ⊢  B→C)
 *   3. Abduction   (A→B, B   ⊢  A with reduced confidence)
 *   4. Revision    (TV₁ ⊕ TV₂  →  revised TV)
 *   5. Conjunction (A ∧ B)
 *   6. Disjunction (A ∨ B)
 *   7. Negation    (¬A)
 *   8. Modus Ponens (A, A→B  ⊢  B)
 *   9. Similarity  (A ≡ B via symmetric evidence)
 *  10. Intensional Inheritance (attribute-based)
 *
 * Membrane mapping
 * ────────────────
 *   Membrane 0  : Environment (external evidence stream)
 *   Membrane 1  : PLN engine root
 *   Membrane 2  : Forward-chaining chamber   (deduction / modus ponens)
 *   Membrane 3  : Backward-chaining chamber  (abduction / goal-driven)
 *   Membrane 4  : Revision chamber           (truth-value merging)
 *   Membrane 5  : Induction chamber          (generalisation)
 *   Membrane 6  : Logical-operator chamber   (AND / OR / NOT)
 *   Membrane 7  : Similarity chamber         (SimilarityLink inference)
 *   Membrane 8  : RR-PLN bridge              (RR salience → PLN TV)
 *
 * Objects
 * ───────
 *   premise_a, premise_b    – input premises (ConceptNode proxies)
 *   rule_deduce             – deduction rule application token
 *   rule_induct             – induction rule application token
 *   rule_abduce             – abduction rule application token
 *   rule_revise             – revision rule application token
 *   rule_and, rule_or,
 *   rule_not                – Boolean-style rule tokens
 *   rule_sim                – similarity rule token
 *   conclusion              – produced conclusion atom
 *   tv_strong, tv_weak      – truth-value strength signals
 *   conf_high, conf_low     – confidence signals
 *   evidence{n}             – counted evidence units
 *   rr_salience_high        – high salience signal from RR engine
 *   rr_salience_low         – low salience signal
 *   rr_affordance           – affordance signal from RR engine
 *   inference_done          – cycle-complete signal
 *   chain_fwd, chain_bwd    – chaining direction tokens
 */</span>

@model&lt;opencog_pln&gt;

@include <span class="hljs-string">"opencog_atomspace.pli"</span>

@Θ-system pln_inference_engine <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>

    <span class="hljs-comment">/* ── Affordance space ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */</span>
    @affordances = <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>

Here is the raw content<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span>
Contents of https<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span><span class="hljs-comment">//raw.githubusercontent.com/ReZorg/plingua/master/examples/opencog/opencog_openpsi.pli:</span>
<span class="hljs-comment">/*
 * opencog_openpsi.pli
 *
 * OpenPsi (Open Psychological-Sigma) implemented as a pure P-Lingua
 * membrane computing system integrated into the relevance-realization (RR)
 * architecture.
 *
 * OpenPsi is OpenCog's motivational and action-selection system.  Inspired
 * by Dietrich Dörner's Psi theory, it models an agent's internal drives,
 * goals, and modulators that govern behaviour selection.
 *
 * OpenPsi mechanisms modelled
 * ────────────────────────────
 *   1. Demand system          – built-in drives (competence, integrity, etc.)
 *   2. Goal hierarchy         – structured goal-subgoal relationships
 *   3. Modulator system       – affect modulators (activation, resolution, etc.)
 *   4. Action selection       – choose action that best satisfies current demands
 *   5. Precondition checking  – verify context before action execution
 *   6. Satisfaction update    – update demand after successful action
 *   7. Emotional state        – composite affect from modulators
 *   8. RR-OpenPsi bridge      – salience guides demand urgency
 *
 * Membrane mapping
 * ────────────────
 *   Membrane 0  : External world / action results
 *   Membrane 1  : OpenPsi root (motivational system)
 *   Membrane 2  : Demand membrane
 *   Membrane 3  : Goal membrane (goal hierarchy)
 *   Membrane 4  : Modulator membrane
 *   Membrane 5  : Action-selection membrane
 *   Membrane 6  : Precondition-checking membrane
 *   Membrane 7  : Satisfaction-update membrane
 *   Membrane 8  : Emotional-state membrane
 *   Membrane 9  : RR-OpenPsi bridge
 *
 * Objects
 * ───────
 *   demand_energy{n}     – energy demand level (survival, competence, …)
 *   demand_food{n}       – food/resource demand
 *   demand_social{n}     – social interaction demand
 *   demand_integrity{n}  – integrity/consistency demand
 *   goal_active          – currently pursued goal token
 *   goal_achieved        – successfully completed goal
 *   goal_failed          – failed goal
 *   sub_goal             – decomposed sub-goal token
 *   action_token         – pending action
 *   action_done          – executed action signal
 *   precond_met          – precondition satisfied
 *   precond_failed       – precondition not met
 *   satisfaction{n}      – demand satisfaction units
 *   mod_activation{n}    – activation modulator level
 *   mod_resolution{n}    – resolution modulator level
 *   mod_selection{n}     – selection threshold modulator
 *   mod_securing{n}      – securing rate modulator
 *   emotion_joy          – composite positive affect
 *   emotion_fear         – composite negative affect
 *   emotion_boredom      – low-arousal negative affect
 *   rr_salience_high, rr_salience_low  – RR bridge signals
 *   rr_affordance        – RR affordance signal
 */</span>

@model&lt;opencog_openpsi&gt;

@include <span class="hljs-string">"op

&lt;note&gt;Content truncated. Call the fetch tool with a start_index of 2800 to get more content.&lt;/note&gt;

data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 3 — PLN + OpenPsi in nn.nn's PLingua dialect (v2.4)</h2>
<p>Following recommendation <strong>B</strong>: deepen the cognitive stack on the <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code> foundation, using ReZorg/plingua's <code node="[object Object]">opencog_pln.pli</code> / <code node="[object Object]">opencog_openpsi.pli</code> as semantic references (different dialect — ours is the pure <code node="[object Object]">@module</code>/<code node="[object Object]">@rules</code> educational syntax).</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/pln.pli</code> — Probabilistic Logic Networks over the AtomSpace: deduction, induction, abduction, revision, conjunction, disjunction, negation, modus ponens (strength/confidence formulas, ×100 ints)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/openpsi.pli</code> — Dörner Psi motivational system: demands, goal hierarchy, modulators, action selection, satisfaction update → drives a9nn <code node="[object Object]">emotion_unit</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Tests for both in <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Demos in <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code> (PLN inference chain, OpenPsi cycle, + a supply-chain worked example)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Register both in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code> module_registry</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Update <code node="[object Object]">README.md</code> (sections, counts, nD-extension + parallel-ledger note)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> validate.sh passes; secret scan; CodeQL; commit/push; memory</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: integers ×100. No change to shared XOR fixture — cross-port consistency preserved.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">No changes to commit. class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'863,875p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/pli/README.md
| `transformer_decoder.pli` | Full transformer decoder with causal masking: masked self-attention, cross-attention, masked multi-head attention, decoder blocks/stacks, sinusoidal positional encoding, full encoder-decoder transformer, greedy decoding |

<span class="hljs-comment">## Extensions (v2.3)</span>

| Module | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `atomspace.pli` | OpenCog-style hypergraph knowledge base: atom nodes/links as membranes (structural nesting), truth values with count-based revision, attention values (STI/LTI), ECAN attention spreading as conserved-currency antiport exchange, Hebbian learning, pattern matching, derived attentional focus. Mirrors `lang/a9nn/AtomSpace.lua`. |
| `a9nn.pli` | NNECCO cognitive agent: Echo State Reservoir membrane (leaky-integrator neurons, spectral radius), 12-beat EchoBeats loop phase-locked by a PC register, emotion processing unit (8 channels), consciousness layers L0–L3 with loss-driven meta-cognition, prioritised episodic memory, parallel LLaMA pool (least-load antiport dispatch, stub mode), hardware-style registers R0–R4/PC/STA. Mirrors `lang/a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua`. |

<span class="hljs-comment">### Weight Interchange Format</span>

`integration.pli` exposes `save_weights` / `load_weights` rules that round-trip
trained models via a simple record format shared conceptually with the other
&lt;shellId: 7 completed with <span class="hljs-built_in">exit</span> code 0&gt;
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 3 — PLN + OpenPsi in nn.nn's PLingua dialect (v2.4)</h2>
<p>Deepened the cognitive stack on <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code>, using ReZorg/plingua's <code node="[object Object]">opencog_pln.pli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">opencog_openpsi.pli</code> as semantic references (different dialect — ours is the pure <code node="[object Object]">@module</code>/<code node="[object Object]">@rules</code> educational syntax).</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/pln.pli</code> — PLN over the AtomSpace: deduction, induction, abduction, revision, conjunction, disjunction, negation, modus ponens (SimpleTruthValue strength/confidence, ×100 ints); maximally-parallel <code node="[object Object]">pln_forward_chain</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/openpsi.pli</code> — Dörner Psi system: demands (competence/integrity/exploration/affiliation) accrue→urgency, goal activation, argmax action selection, satisfaction feedback, modulators → drive a9nn <code node="[object Object]">emotion_unit</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> 8 tests in <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code> (46 → 54)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> 3 demos in <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code> (19 → 22): PLN inference chain, OpenPsi cycle, <strong>parallel supply-chain ledger (1000 entities settle in O(1) steps)</strong></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Register <code node="[object Object]">pln</code> + <code node="[object Object]">openpsi</code> in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code> module_registry (v2.4)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">README.md</code>: feature bullets, PLN/OpenPsi API sections, roadmap checkboxes, v2.4 table, counts (54 tests / 22 demos), coverage bullets, <strong>"Looking ahead: nD membranes and parallel ledgers"</strong> note (<code node="[object Object]">.dli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">.vli</code> candidates)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code> passes all 27 files; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A (no analyzable languages)</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: integers ×100. No change to the shared XOR fixture — cross-port consistency preserved.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">Committed and pushed<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> feat(pli)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> add PLN inference + OpenPsi motivational system (v2<span class="hljs-number">.4</span>)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> parallel-ledger demo

<p>1 row(s) updated.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="CopilotMessageBubble-module__container__oW7JC"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf CopilotMessageBubble-module__text__ADukm" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><p>Phase 3 complete ✅ — <strong>PLN + OpenPsi</strong> are implemented, tested, documented, and pushed.</p>
<h2>What was built (v2.4)</h2>
<p><strong><code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/pln.pli</code></strong> — Probabilistic Logic Networks over the AtomSpace, using ReZorg's <code node="[object Object]">opencog_pln.pli</code> as the semantic reference (translated into our pure <code node="[object Object]">@module</code>/<code node="[object Object]">@rules</code> dialect):</p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]">Deduction, induction, abduction, revision, conjunction, disjunction, negation, modus ponens — each a membrane rule with proper SimpleTruthValue strength/confidence formulas (e.g., deduction <code node="[object Object]">s = sAB·sBC/100</code>, abduction confidence attenuated ×50/100 as tentative)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong><code node="[object Object]">pln_forward_chain</code></strong> — the key win: forward chaining is a <em>single maximally-parallel transition</em>, not a loop</li>
</ul>
<p><strong><code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/openpsi.pli</code></strong> — Dörner's Psi motivational system:</p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]">Demands (competence/integrity/exploration/affiliation) accrue tension → become urgent → activate goals → argmax action selection → satisfaction feedback lowers tension</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>The bridge that matters</strong>: modulators map onto the a9nn <code node="[object Object]">emotion_unit</code>, so <em>motivation is felt</em> (urgent exploration → curiosity ↑) and action outcomes feed back as reward. This closes the agent's cognitive cycle: it now has <strong>knowledge (AtomSpace), inference (PLN), and motivation (OpenPsi)</strong>.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Tests/demos/registry/docs:</strong> 8 tests (46→<strong>54</strong>), 3 demos (19→<strong>22</strong>), both registered in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code>, README updated. All 27 <code node="[object Object]">.pli</code> files pass <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code>; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A; XOR fixture untouched (cross-port consistency preserved).</p>
<h2>Your supply-chain question — answered with a demo 🎯</h2>
<p><strong>Yes, absolutely.</strong> Demo 22 (<code node="[object Object]">parallel_ledger_demo</code>) shows exactly this: a chart of accounts for a <strong>1000-entity supply chain</strong> where each entity is a membrane holding <code node="[object Object]">account{acct, balance}</code> objects. Because P-System rules are <em>maximally parallel</em>, <strong>every entity's double-entry transfers settle in the same step</strong> — settlement is <strong>O(1) membrane steps, not O(n)</strong> entities. Double-entry conservation holds by construction. This is arguably the most natural real-world fit for membrane computing there is.</p>
<h2>On the nD extension</h2>
<p>Both <strong><code node="[object Object]">.dli</code></strong> and <strong><code node="[object Object]">.vli</code></strong> are free (only <code node="[object Object]">.pli</code> is used in nn.nn; <code node="[object Object]">.mli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">.rli</code> belong to the ReZorg platform). The README now documents this direction — making dimension a <em>parameter</em> rather than a fixed grid, which pairs naturally with <code node="[object Object]">lang/pl/nn_nd.pl</code> (rank-parametric convolution already exists there in Prolog).</p>
<hr>
<h3>Where next?</h3>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Unify the cognitive cycle</strong>: wire PLN + OpenPsi <em>into</em> <code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code>'s EchoBeats (REASON←PLN, EMOTE←OpenPsi modulators) so the agent truly reasons and wants — the <code node="[object Object]">opencog_unified_agi.pli</code> equivalent</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong><code node="[object Object]">.dli</code>/<code node="[object Object]">.vli</code> prototype</strong>: define the rank-parametric nD membrane dialect</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>M-Lingua spatial</strong> (Option C): morphogenetic tiles + Cytos XML → Unity visualization</li>
</ul>
<p>Want me to <strong>unify the agent's cognitive cycle</strong> (PLN+OpenPsi+AtomSpace fully integrated into EchoBeats) next?</p></div></div><div class="SessionFooter-module__footer__uEbNi
